# Paper-aligned ablation report

The original paper results and the new independent ablation fits are separate evidence sets. A completed fit is reused only for its exact frozen arm, seed, fold and configuration. Changing a learned input requires retraining.

Primary statistical differences use common complete days; all-retained daily headline metrics include partial boundary dates as in the paper. These horizons are labeled separately. No missing bar is manufactured.

## RQ1

RQ1: Does the combination of Ichimoku and vision agentic portfolio improve overall cumulative net return and annualized daily-return Sharpe relative to Bitcoin buy-and-hold, equal-weight cryptocurrency, large-cap equal weight, and top-three momentum over the common walk-forward validation period? In addition, does combining Ichimoku technical signals and chart-based vision improve cumulative net return and annualized daily-return Sharpe relative to Ichimoku-only and vision-only configurations?

H1: The combined technical and vision configuration is expected to beat both the technical-only and vision-only configurations on cumulative net return and on annualized Sharpe ratio from daily returns. In a supplementary comparison with regime and sector adjustments turned off in all three arms, the claim is treated as statistically confirmed only if, against both single-signal arms, the paired confidence interval for the Sharpe difference is above zero and the Holm-adjusted p-value is below .05.

**H1 NOT STATISTICALLY ESTABLISHED**

Combined-minus-single-signal effects: [{"b":"FIXED_I","metric":"sharpe","effect":-0.2090245645,"ci_low":-0.7038150673,"ci_high":0.1606582857,"p_holm":0.4847576212},{"b":"FIXED_I","metric":"total_return","effect":-0.6480666754,"ci_low":-8.2629801402,"ci_high":0.9048090673,"p_holm":1.0},{"b":"FIXED_V","metric":"sharpe","effect":0.0396504876,"ci_low":-0.0178252318,"ci_high":0.1279857425,"p_holm":0.4847576212},{"b":"FIXED_V","metric":"total_return","effect":0.13332531,"ci_low":-0.0364295854,"ci_high":1.6744582295,"p_holm":1.0}]. ## RQ2

RQ2: Does the regime-and-sector portfolio improve net risk-adjusted performance and downside behavior relative to the unmodified technical–vision baseline, and what do the configured regime and LLM-layer studies reveal about that effect? 

H2: Holding the signal inputs, rebalancing schedule and execution assumptions the same, adding regime and sector adjustments is expected to raise cumulative net return and annualized Sharpe and lower maximum drawdown compared with the base process.

**H2 DIRECTIONAL EXPECTATION MET**

## RQ3

RQ3: Does the constrained SAC implementation improve risk-adjusted performance and maximum drawdown outcomes relative to the weekly agentic baseline across all sixteen validation windows?

H3: Relative to the weekly agentic baseline, the complete SAC implementation will have a higher annualized daily-return Sharpe, a smaller full-period maximum drawdown, and lower local maximum drawdown in a majority of the sixteen windows. Cumulative net return, volatility, turnover, costs, and paired uncertainty intervals provide supporting evidence. Because SAC refreshes targets every fifteen minutes, this expectation concerns the complete implementation rather than the isolated effect of learned exposure scaling.

**H3 DIRECTIONAL EXPECTATION NOT MET IN EVERY SEED**

## RQ4

RQ4: How does the original SAC overlay’s mean exposure vary across the five reported drawdown bands (below 5%, 5%–below 10%, 10%–below 15%, 15%–below 20%, and at least 20%), and what exposure differences and limit violations occur across its sixteen validation windows? 

H4: Mean cryptocurrency exposure is expected to be lower during severe drawdowns (at least 20%) than during minimal drawdowns (below 5%) across the original sixteen windows. The severe-versus-minimal exposure contrast is interpreted descriptively. Compliance with the programmed exposure limits tests implementation behavior, not whether SAC learned effective risk management. The original summaries pair exposure with post-return fold drawdown; they do not by themselves identify a response to pre-action risk.

**H4 DESCRIPTIVE EXPECTATION NOT MET IN EVERY RUN**

independent refits of the original SAC protocol: severe-minus-minimal exposure [-0.05200074719133296, 0.010805214446723443, -0.005772741990488273]; limit violations [0.0, 0.0, 0.0]. All five bands and all windows include observation counts; pre-action bands are a separate diagnostic. Programmed-bound compliance is an implementation check. New refits do not reconstruct missing historical weights. New-refit chart input audit: {"invalid_processed_price_bars": 978, "invalid_processed_volume_bars": 0, "raw_price_replacements": 0, "raw_volume_replacements": 0, "unresolved_price_bars": 978, "unresolved_volume_bars": 0}; label audit: {"selected_charts": 25536, "invalid_endpoints_or_empty_context": 28, "recomputed_label_changes": 0, "rule": "Ineligible labels never enter teacher or student training/validation; empty-context inference is neutral."}. Raw replacements use the same timestamp. Unresolved candles/volumes are marked missing; invalid label endpoints are excluded from vision training/validation. Portfolio returns and original feature frames were not modified, so this does not repair or certify original price preprocessing. Detailed evidence: chart_input_audit/summary.csv, affected_bars.csv and selected_chart_labels.parquet.

Evidence: exposure_five_bands.csv; exposure_severe_minus_minimal.csv

## Interpretation

“Not statistically established” means the prespecified test did not demonstrate the effect. It does not prove a zero effect. Incomplete computations identify work still required. Historical data cannot be made prospective by rerunning the code.

Minimal plan: 11 configurations, three seeds, all sixteen original folds, 144 SAC fits with a predeclared 36,000-step budget per fit (archived budget: 90,000). This reduced budget is uniform across variants and must traverse every training bar at least once. Architecture, optimizer, original reward and original testing functions are retained; convergence is not presumed. Daily price-only charts use a direct 224px candle/volume raster; teacher/student losses and 10/8 epochs are retained. Chart representation and sampling differ from the archived image pipeline. GA, DDPM and separate portfolio-agent ablations are excluded. Three seeds estimate limited training variability; significance and power are not guaranteed. Tests remain conditional on one previously examined market history.

RQ1 RQ1: Does the combination of Ichimoku and vision agentic portfolio improve overall cumulative net return and annualized daily-return Sharpe relative to Bitcoin buy-and-hold, equal-weight cryptocurrency, large-cap equal weight, and top-three momentum over the common walk-forward validation period? In addition, does combining Ichimoku technical signals and chart-based vision improve cumulative net return and annualized daily-return Sharpe relative to Ichimoku-only and vision-only configurations? H1: The combined technical and vision configuration is expected to beat both the technical-only and vision-only configurations on cumulative net return and on annualized Sharpe ratio from daily returns. In a supplementary comparison with regime and sector adjustments turned off in all three arms, the claim is treated as statistically confirmed only if, against both single-signal arms, the paired confidence interval for the Sharpe difference is above zero and the Holm-adjusted p-value is below .05. RQ3 RQ3: Does the constrained SAC implementation improve risk-adjusted performance and maximum drawdown outcomes relative to the weekly agentic baseline across all sixteen validation windows? H3: Relative to the weekly agentic baseline, the complete SAC implementation will have a higher annualized daily-return Sharpe, a smaller full-period maximum drawdown, and lower local maximum drawdown in a majority of the sixteen windows. limitations_and_resolution_status

Full table in limitations_and_resolution_status.csv

issue implemented_evidence remaining_boundary
training seeds Independent per-arm/seed/fold fits and paired seed/time bootstrap Completed study still shares one observed market history; folds are not independent seeds.
cadence confound Weekly comparison plus cadence-matched fixed exposure comparison Original H3 concerns complete implementations, not causal isolation of exposure.
turnover and fold resets Original accounting deliberately retained for controlled comparisons Drifted-holdings turnover and uninterrupted execution are not established by this study.
transaction costs Fixed-decision fee sensitivity on completed paths No order book, exchange fills or market impact validation.
coverage Exact fold/bar counts; partial dates identified Original dropped boundary interval is not fabricated; year ends at the observed endpoint.
processed prices Original price audit and pre-test price-only vision fits Original clipping/imputation and point-in-time feature uncertainty remain.
large drawdown Original SAC versus weekly and cadence-matched controls, all folds Original 80–100% exposure constraint retained; no hard drawdown cap or guaranteed improvement.
historical agents Actual cached decisions used; joint-layer comparison and decision records exported Current replay cannot certify unavailable historical prompts, model cutoff or inputs.
supplemental components GA, DDPM and separate portfolio agents excluded by the minimal design No claim of new execution or validation of excluded components.
prospective execution Explicit research boundary Paper trading and live validation were not performed.
